## Run 48: `store_gc_cli` tests with the Casita GC positive and Rust-cache negative, passed

- Reported by: CLI owner (`bg_839`; the owner cited `proc://bg_839`). Not rerun
  by the recorder. The output below is the async result of `bg_839`.
- Source: `tests/store_gc_cli.rs#1C2E`, the owner's last edit (20:23:26 UTC),
  followed by rustfmt at 20:23:33 UTC. The owner read the file with the same
  snapshot at 20:26:29 UTC, and so did the recorder. No agent edited or rewrote
  a file between 20:23:34 UTC and the end of the run (20:25:39 UTC); the
  recorder checked every session log for edit and write calls, shell commands,
  and eval writes in that window.
- Identity: captured at 20:23:50 UTC by a separate
  `git rev-parse HEAD && git status --short` (artifact://4952, SHA-256
  `5a84897b95119ee528b74cfc85ebe4d44af91d76e20f72808e175b7c125b2b84`). It shows
  HEAD `7ec5177718a6950297e04eb4eb957a10b02e23ce` and the status lines of Run
  47 (Run 32's plus ` M crates/crunch-store/tests/authority_source_policy.rs`),
  without the branch line. The captures at 20:10:24, 20:12:41, and 20:20:28 UTC
  and the one after the run, at 20:26:16 UTC (artifact://4959), have the same
  bytes. Only `bg_838` ran between the capture and this command.
- Command (mantle repository root), started 20:25:13 UTC:
  `nix develop --offline --no-write-lock-file --command env -u CARGO_HOME SNIX_BUILD_SANDBOX_SHELL=/bin/sh CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/home/brittonr/.cargo-target cargo test --locked --offline --config .cargo/vendor-config.toml -q -p mantle --test store_gc_cli -- --nocapture`
- Exit code: 0; the async result has no non-zero exit footer.
- Fixtures run: 12 passed, 0 filtered out. With `-q` the harness prints one dot
  per test; the file has 12 tests, the 11 of Run 29 and
  `casita_store_gc_cli_collects_unpinned_signed_output_and_preserves_retained_nar`
  (`tests/store_gc_cli.rs:302-449`), whose `CASITA_GC_CLI_EVIDENCE` line is in
  the output. That test:
  - Admits two signed content-addressed outputs under `casita` through the
    library, with a policy file listing their signer, and exports both.
    `store pin` retains the first through the CLI.
  - `--json store gc` (dry run) reports one retained root and one candidate,
    the unretained output, and removes no export.
  - Negative: the test then creates
    `<state-dir>/rust-unit-cache/retained-cache-state`. `store gc` and
    `store gc --execute --plan-id <id>` both fail with
    `casita-rust-cache-unsupported`. Afterward the marker keeps its bytes,
    `gc-roots.json` keeps its bytes, neither `casita-gc-fence.json` nor
    `casita-gc-fence.progress` exists, both exports still point at their
    content, and `store list` returns the same paths as before. The test then
    removes the marker and the directory; `remove_dir` succeeds only on an
    empty directory, so the failed commands added nothing there.
  - Positive: with no `rust-unit-cache` entry,
    `--json store gc --execute --plan-id <id>` with the same plan completes,
    with no failed operations, the planned candidates, and operations that
    include `casita-root-removal` and `casita-collection`. `store info` for the
    retained output reports its NAR size, NAR SHA-256, and signatures
    unchanged; `store verify --trusted-public-keys <signer>` reports
    `trusted_signatures=1/1`; its export still points at its content;
    `store list` shows only it; `store info` for the collected output finds no
    PathInfo; and its export is gone.
  - Code path (`src/store_cmd.rs#0A2A`): under `casita`, `cmd_store_gc_action`
    checks for a `rust-unit-cache` entry before it takes the mutation guard or
    opens the store (lines 297-300), and `plan_rust_cache_retention` returns
    without calling `RustCache::open_async` (lines 365-371).
    `RustCache::open_async` itself fails for `casita`
    (`crates/crunch-rust-cache/src/lib.rs:253-255`), so the successful
    execution also shows that the Rust unit cache was not opened.
- Coverage: T3.6 is checked from this run. The positive plans and executes
  output GC with no Rust unit cache state, without opening the Rust unit cache,
  removes the unretained output at the plan that selected it, and leaves the
  retained output verifiable. The negative fails with
  `casita-rust-cache-unsupported` for planning and for execution, before any
  fence or root removal, and leaves the Rust-cache state unchanged. The state
  is a marker file; `store gc` treats any `rust-unit-cache` entry as Rust unit
  cache state. The other 11 tests pass again at the current source.

## Run 52: `bootstrap --fetch` under `casita` with the durable signer, one-off interactive smoke, passed

- Reported by: CLI owner, as a summary in a message. The recorder took the
  cells and their outputs from the owner's session log and did not rerun them.
  This is a throwaway smoke run from the owner's eval kernel, not a permanent
  fixture.
- Source: the binary `/home/brittonr/.cargo-target/debug/mantle`, built by the
  owner's `cargo build` started at 20:52:27 UTC (`bg_853`, 79.72 seconds, no
  non-zero exit footer; command below) from the sources after the owner's edits
  that ended at 20:52:16 UTC. Those edits add `src/signing_key.rs#3831`, which
  takes over the signing-key helpers from `src/build_cmd.rs`, move their
  callers to it, and change `src/bootstrap.rs#513C`: under `casita`,
  `fetch_raw_seed` calls
  `crate::signing_key::load_or_generate_signing_keypair(None, request.state_dir, false)`
  after `preflight_backend_identity` and the store mutation guard, and under
  `snix` it keeps `crunch_build::generate_keypair()`
  (`src/bootstrap.rs:816-851`). No agent edited a source file between 20:52:16
  UTC and the end of the smoke (20:56:07 UTC), and no other Cargo command ran
  after the build started. `src/bootstrap.rs` and `src/signing_key.rs` still
  had those snapshots when the recorder wrote this entry; the formatting pass
  at 21:17 UTC (see the note after Run 55) later reformatted
  `src/bootstrap.rs`.
- Identity: none was captured. The last capture, at 20:31:23 UTC
  (artifact://4987, Run 51), predates the owner's edits.
- Commands: 13 cells in the owner's Python eval kernel, run between 20:53:06
  and 20:56:06 UTC and shown verbatim below with their outputs (a 30-second
  sleep cell is omitted). Each `boot_run` call starts a new `mantle` process
  with `CRUNCH_CONFIG_DIR` removed from its environment. The fixture copies
  `bootstrap/seed-legacy.ncl` into a temporary working directory and replaces
  its pinned `https://musl.cc/` URL and hash with a local `file://` tarball and
  that tree's recursive SHA-256, so no network is used.
- Exit codes of the `mantle` commands: both `bootstrap --fetch` runs 0, the
  `snix` attempt 3, and the attempt under a foreign-only policy 1;
  `store list`, `store info`, `store verify`, and `attest key-show` were
  asserted to exit 0. Three cells failed on the owner's own assertions and were
  rewritten: the generated seed names `/crunch/store/`, not `/nix/store/` (cell
  4); a key-length check (cell 6); and `store list` without
  `--store-prefix /crunch/store`, which failed with `store-identity-mismatch`
  because `bootstrap` records the `/crunch/store` prefix (cell 7).
- What the smoke shows:
  - In a new state directory without a policy file, `bootstrap --fetch` fetches
    the raw seed, builds the reduced provider, writes `seed.ncl` with
    `/crunch/store/` paths, and creates `<state-dir>/signing-key` with mode
    0600.
  - In new processes, `store list` shows one path, the raw seed (NAR size
    133,816); `store info` shows one signature, by the generated key; and
    `store verify --trusted-public-keys <its public key>` reports
    `trusted_signatures=1/1` (NAR SHA-256
    `e91bcae514d4712b918857f5cd369dc69859712c12e1f41a1f8d54a16feabbb3`).
  - A second `bootstrap --fetch` reports the raw seed and the reduced provider
    cached; the signing key, the seed file, and the PathInfo keep their bytes.
  - `bootstrap --fetch` with `--store-backend snix` on this Casita state
    directory fails with `store-backend-mismatch` (exit 3). No seed file is
    written, and the identity file and the signing key keep their bytes.
  - With a policy file that lists only a foreign key, `bootstrap --fetch` fails
    (exit 1) because the lookup of the already published raw seed fails with
    `casita-signer-untrusted`. No seed file is written, and the policy file and
    the signing key keep their bytes. After the policy file is removed,
    `store verify` again reports `trusted_signatures=1/1`.
- Coverage: no task is checked. This is the first observation of
  `bootstrap --fetch` under `casita` with the durable signer. For T2.14 it
  shows the new bootstrap positive; its policy case reads an existing output
  instead of failing a new admission, so it is not the listed bootstrap
  negative. T4.3 gains a one-off reuse observation, and selection T3.2 a
  one-off observation of the recorded-backend mismatch through `bootstrap`.

Cell 1, 20:53:06 UTC: fixture: raw tree, recursive hash, local tarball, and a copied `bootstrap/seed-legacy.ncl` that points at it.

```text
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile, tarfile, subprocess, os, json
repo_bootstrap = Path('/home/brittonr/git/OnixResearch/mantle')
boot_fixture = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='mantle-casita-legacy-bootstrap-')
boot_root = Path(boot_fixture.name)
raw = boot_root/'raw'
(raw/'bin').mkdir(parents=True)
(raw/'include').mkdir()
(raw/'include'/'stdio.h').write_text('/* offline bootstrap smoke fixture */\n')
(raw/'x86_64-linux-musl'/'lib').mkdir(parents=True)
(raw/'x86_64-linux-musl'/'lib'/'libgcc_s.so.1').write_bytes(b'fixture-libgcc-s\n')
for tool in ('x86_64-linux-musl-gcc','x86_64-linux-musl-g++','ar','ld'):
    executable = raw/'bin'/tool
    executable.write_text('#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n')
    executable.chmod(0o755)
(raw/'share'/'locale').mkdir(parents=True)
(raw/'share'/'locale'/'dropped-junk').write_bytes(b'X'*131072)
nh = subprocess.run(['nix','hash','path','--sri',str(raw)],cwd=boot_root,text=True,capture_output=True,timeout=30,check=True)
raw_hash = nh.stdout.strip()
tarball=boot_root/'offline-raw.tgz'
with tarfile.open(tarball,'w:gz') as archive:
    archive.add(raw,arcname='offline-raw-v1')
provider_text=(repo_bootstrap/'bootstrap'/'seed-legacy.ncl').read_text()
assert provider_text.count('https://musl.cc/x86_64-linux-musl-native.tgz') == 1
assert provider_text.count('sha256-ZtQZncMvugqmS7OMvMtdhY5MMtem4KXBhamxWbHUDkY=') == 1
provider_text=provider_text.replace('https://musl.cc/x86_64-linux-musl-native.tgz',tarball.as_uri()).replace('sha256-ZtQZncMvugqmS7OMvMtdhY5MMtem4KXBhamxWbHUDkY=',raw_hash)
(boot_root/'bootstrap').mkdir()
(boot_root/'bootstrap'/'seed-legacy.ncl').write_text(provider_text)
(boot_root/'store').mkdir()
(boot_root/'state').mkdir()
print(json.dumps({'fixture_root':str(boot_root),'nar_hash':raw_hash,'tarball_size':tarball.stat().st_size,'raw_files':len(list(raw.rglob('*')))}))
```

Cell 2, 20:53:22 UTC: runner: each call starts a new `mantle` process under `casita` without `CRUNCH_CONFIG_DIR`.

```text
import re,hashlib
boot_cli = Path('/home/brittonr/.cargo-target/debug/mantle')
def boot_run(*args, backend='casita', cwd=boot_root, max_seconds=80):
    env = dict(os.environ)
    env.pop('CRUNCH_CONFIG_DIR', None)
    result = subprocess.run([str(boot_cli),'--state-dir',str(boot_root/'state'),'--store',str(boot_root/'store'),'--store-backend',backend,*args], cwd=cwd, env=env, text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=max_seconds)
    return result
print('Offline bootstrap CLI smoke runner ready for corrected executable; fixture NAR', raw_hash)
```

Cell 8, 20:55:13 UTC: runner with `--store-prefix /crunch/store`; `store list`.

```text
def boot_run(*args, backend='casita', cwd=boot_root, max_seconds=80, prefix='/crunch/store'):
    env = dict(os.environ)
    env.pop('CRUNCH_CONFIG_DIR', None)
    return subprocess.run([str(boot_cli),'--state-dir',str(boot_root/'state'),'--store',str(boot_root/'store'),'--store-prefix',prefix,'--store-backend',backend,*args], cwd=cwd, env=env, text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=max_seconds)
listed=boot_run('--json','store','list')
assert listed.returncode==0,listed.stderr
print(json.dumps({'bootstrap_exit':first_boot.returncode,'seed_sha256':hashlib.sha256(first_seed.encode()).hexdigest(),'signing_key_mode':oct((boot_root/'state'/'signing-key').stat().st_mode&0o777),'trusted_signer_name':trusted_key.split(':',1)[0],'list_stdout':listed.stdout[:1300]}))
```

- Reported by: CLI owner (`bg_860`, artifact://5117). Not rerun by the
  recorder. The wall time comes from the async result of `bg_860`.
- Source: `tests/integration_build.rs#6895`, the owner's last edit (21:10:08
  UTC), which added a `store list` check on the foreign state before the
  refusal; the test is `tests/integration_build.rs:1083-1217`. The binary
  includes `src/bootstrap.rs#513C`, `src/signing_key.rs#3831`, and
  `src/main.rs#A864` (the owner's 21:05:10 UTC change described under Run 54).
  No agent edited a source file between 21:10:09 UTC and the end of the run
  (21:11:02 UTC), and the recorder read the test afterward with the same
  snapshot.
- Command (mantle repository root), started 21:10:14 UTC. It prints the git
  identity itself, right before the test:
  `git rev-parse HEAD && git status --short --branch && nix develop --offline --no-write-lock-file --command env -u CARGO_HOME SNIX_BUILD_SANDBOX_SHELL=/bin/sh CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/home/brittonr/.cargo-target cargo test --locked --offline --config .cargo/vendor-config.toml -q -p mantle --test integration_build casita_legacy_bootstrap_fetch_uses_durable_local_signer_and_exclusive_policy -- --exact --nocapture`.
  HEAD is `7ec5177718a6950297e04eb4eb957a10b02e23ce`; the status lines are Run
  47's plus ` M src/artifact_cmd.rs`, ` M src/lib.rs`,
  ` M src/release_attestation.rs`, and `?? src/signing_key.rs`, from the
  signing-key extraction.
- Exit code: 0; the output has no non-zero exit footer.
- Fixtures run: 1 passed, 14 filtered out:
  `casita_legacy_bootstrap_fetch_uses_durable_local_signer_and_exclusive_policy`.
  It builds a small raw tree, its recursive SHA-256, and a local `file://`
  tarball, and copies `bootstrap/seed-legacy.ncl` into a temporary working
  directory with the pinned URL and hash replaced, so no network is used. Every
  command runs in a new `mantle` process under `casita`, with
  `--store-prefix /crunch/store` and without `CRUNCH_CONFIG_DIR`.
  - Positive, in a new state directory without a policy file:
    `bootstrap --fetch` succeeds and builds the reduced provider, the seed file
    names `/crunch/store/` paths, `<state-dir>/signing-key` exists with mode
    0600, `store list` shows exactly one path, and
    `store verify --trusted-public-keys <that key's verifying key>` reports
    `trusted_signatures=1/1`. A second `bootstrap --fetch` reports both outputs
    cached, and the signing key and the seed file keep their bytes.
  - Negative, in another new state directory whose policy file lists only a
    different fixture key: `store list` succeeds with no path;
    `bootstrap --fetch` fails with `casita-signer-untrusted`; no seed file is
    written; the policy file keeps its bytes; `store list` again succeeds with
    no path; and no `*-musl-gcc-raw` export appears. Casita `store list`
    verifies every output root under the policy, so a published root would have
    made it fail.
- Coverage: T2.14's bootstrap positive and negative pass, including the check
  that no root was published, which T2.14 requires of every negative; so does
  the spec scenario "Bootstrap fetch without a policy file". No failing-before
  run was recorded. T2.3 and T2.14 stay open for the other trust items.
- Earlier runs of this test, superseded: `bg_857` at 21:03:27 UTC on `#4C9D`
  (no identity captured; passed), and `bg_859` at 21:08:23 UTC on `#038E`
  (identity printed inline, with the same status lines as this run; passed;
  artifact://5114, SHA-256
  `3aac691ab4082c8b6ae4277ece55eeb9627908d28b4e29af629c75e7ab2946e1`). The
  owner added the pre-refusal `store list` check after them.

## Run 54: bootstrap offline source preflight reads the selected state directory, one-off smoke, passed

- Reported by: CLI owner, as a summary in a message. The recorder took the two
  cells and their outputs from the owner's session log and did not rerun them.
  The cells reuse the fixture directory and runner of Run 52.
- Source: the binary `/home/brittonr/.cargo-target/debug/mantle`, built by the
  owner's `cargo build` started at 21:06:11 UTC (`bg_858`, 99.23 seconds, no
  non-zero exit footer; same command as the Run 52 build) after the owner's
  21:05:10 UTC edit to `src/main.rs` (`#A864`). With
  `--offline-source-preflight`, `bootstrap --fetch` now passes
  `request.state_dir` to
  `source_bundle::bootstrap_legacy_seed_fetch_override_plan`
  (`src/main.rs:8751-8753`); at HEAD it passed `build_cmd::state_dir()`
  (`src/main.rs:8629-8632` at HEAD). No agent edited a source file between
  21:05:11 UTC and the check (21:08:16 UTC); the test file changed at 21:08:04
  UTC, and it is not part of the `mantle` binary.
- Identity: none was captured.
- What the smoke shows: under `casita`, with `--state-dir A` and
  `CRUNCH_STATE_DIR=B`, a malformed source record in A makes
  `bootstrap --fetch --offline-source-preflight` fail (exit 3) with a parse
  error that names the file in A and never names B. With the malformed record
  only in B, the command fails (exit 3) with
  `missing bootstrap provider archive source state`, because A holds no
  records, and never names B's file. Neither run writes the seed file or
  creates an identity record in A.
- Coverage: none; state-directory selection for the offline preflight is not a
  listed task. T4.2 still needs offline source preflight reports that are equal
  across backends, with no network access under `casita`.

```text
    env=dict(os.environ)

assert str(ambient_state) not in with_selected_poison.stderr,with_selected_poison.stderr

assert str(ambient_poison) not in with_ambient_poison.stderr,with_ambient_poison.stderr

assert not (selected_state/'store-identity.json').exists()
```

  compile. No agent edited or rewrote a file between 21:21:14 UTC and the end
  of the run (21:24:30 UTC), and the recorder read the fixture afterward with
  the same snapshot (`repair.rs:1003-1060`).
- Command (mantle repository root), started 21:21:20 UTC. It prints the git
  identity itself, right before the test:
  `git rev-parse HEAD && git status --short --branch && nix develop --offline --no-write-lock-file --command cargo test -q -p crunch-store --lib repair::tests::casita_repair_library_entrypoints_reject_without_publishing -- --exact --nocapture`.
  HEAD is `7ec5177718a6950297e04eb4eb957a10b02e23ce`; the status lines equal
  Run 53's.
- Exit code: 0; the output has no non-zero exit footer.
- Fixtures run: 1 passed, 395 filtered out:
  `repair::tests::casita_repair_library_entrypoints_reject_without_publishing`.
  A stale signed PathInfo is admitted to a Snix store and inspected there. A
  Casita store then opens with its own state and output directories; the test
  writes a sentinel file into each and confirms the path is absent. It
  snapshots both trees, recording every entry with directory names and file
  bytes and excluding nothing, then calls `inspect_final_nar_repair` on the
  Casita store and compares both trees, then calls `execute_final_nar_repair`
  with the Snix inspection and compares both trees again. Both calls fail with
  `casita-repair-final-nar-unsupported`, both trees are byte-identical after
  each call, the path is still absent from the Casita store, and the Snix
  PathInfo is unchanged. The snapshots are taken after the store opened and
  read once, so they include Casita's reader-pin files as they stood then.
- Coverage: T2.10's library refusal item is now fully asserted: neither call
  changes the Casita state or output directory. T2.10 stays open for the CLI
  refusal against an existing state directory as a dry run; the owner extended
  that fixture at 21:21:05 UTC, and its run has not finished.

## Final quality gates, 21:30 to 21:47 UTC, not a fixture run

This section records the repository quality gates that `mantle-quality-gates`
ran after the repair owner released the Rust sources and the shared Cargo
target. The recorder read the logs and the owner's session log and reran
nothing. The logs live under `/tmp` and are not durable, so this section copies
their decisive lines in `grep -n` form (line number, colon, the line verbatim)
and gives every log's size and SHA-256.

- Reported by: `mantle-quality-gates` at 21:47:14 UTC, with the index
  `/tmp/mantle-final-gates-20260930/commands.txt` (3,402 bytes, SHA-256
  `b04b0a40f26396e466415d2deacfbc289d2acd682de8523e40e77c0d074fc9fd`). The
  parent asked the recorder at 21:47:55 UTC to record it in both changes.
- Identity: `00-identity.log` holds the output of
  `git rev-parse HEAD; git status --short --branch` at 21:30:09 UTC, before the
  first check, and `12-final-identity.log` the same command at 21:46:38 UTC,
  after the last. The two files are byte-identical (5,276 bytes, SHA-256
  `3bc929896ec51de673f4cb98d0f11dc3363fe69da3d9970015fa47d737f44b9a`): HEAD
  `7ec5177718a6950297e04eb4eb957a10b02e23ce`, the branch line
  `## main...origin/main`, and 153 status entries. All 155 lines equal those
  captured for Runs 53, 55, and 56.
- Source edit during the gates: at 21:35:12 UTC `mantle-quality-gates` replaced
  lines 925 to 930 of `src/store_cmd.rs` (snapshot `#E578`), the nested `if`
  that the Clippy `collapsible_if` error below prints at log lines 1031 to
  1036. The new lines, as the file reads now:

  ```text
      if let Some(plan) = rust_retention.filter(|_| is_plan_only || gc_evidence.execution_complete) {
          plan.apply(is_plan_only)
              .map_err(|error| RunError::Build(format!("applying Rust cache retention: {error}")))?;
      }
  ```

  `rust_retention` is an `Option<&crunch_rust_cache::RustCacheRetentionPlan>`.
  Both forms call `plan.apply(is_plan_only)` only when it holds a plan and
  `is_plan_only || gc_evidence.execution_complete` is true. The file was
  already modified, so the status lines do not show the edit. The first quality
  run (its job notice arrived at 21:34:05 UTC) and the first `git diff --check`
  finished before the edit; every other command started after it. The session
  logs show no other write to Rust source between the two identity captures;
  the other writes were `docs/store-backends.md` (`mantle-store-docs`, 21:31:29
  UTC) and this recorder's evidence edits.
- Commands: each ran from the repository root inside
  `bash -lc '<command> > <log> 2>&1; rc=$?; printf ...; exit "$rc"'`. Start
  times (UTC), exit codes, and wall times come from the owner's session log:


That log holds 41 lines that start with `error` besides the four
`could not compile` lines, from the `mantle` and `crunch` binaries and their
test targets. One is the `src/store_cmd.rs` error above. The other 40 name
`src/command_input.rs` (15), `src/source_observation.rs` (7),
`src/protected_exec.rs` (5), `src/main.rs` (3), `src/remote_nominal.rs` (2),
`src/ast_grep_evidence.rs` (2), and one each

## Note: backend identity preflight fix, 22:07 to 22:12 UTC, not a test run

Selection Runs 12 to 16, in
`../../add-store-backend-selection/evidence/test-runs-2026-09-30.md`, record a
source fix by `mantle-quality-gates` for a wrong-backend `build` that wrote
`<state-dir>/signing-key` before it failed with `store-backend-mismatch`. The
fix changes `crates/crunch-store/src/handle.rs`, `src/build_cmd.rs`,
`src/main.rs`, `src/self_build.rs`, `src/foreign_import_cmd.rs`, and
`tests/store_gc_cli.rs`, and edits and restores
`crates/crunch-store/src/overlay.rs`; selection Run 13 gives the snapshots.

For a `casita` selection, `StoreConfig::preflight_backend_identity` checks what
it checked before. The old body, as the owner read it at 22:03:26 UTC, rejected
bases with `casita-overlay-unsupported` and then called
`overlay::preflight_store_identity` on the selected directory. The new body
does the same and then checks each base as `snix`, which a `casita` selection
never reaches. New calls run this read-only check before the signing key is
loaded or generated on the build, self-build, and foreign-realization paths.

After the fix, the `store_gc_cli` suite passed 14 of 14 twice (selection Runs
14 and 16), including the tests behind T3.6 (Runs 48 and 49) and the Rust unit
cache rejections of T2.15 (Run 29).
`cargo clippy -p crunch-store --lib --no-deps -- -D warnings`,
`cargo fmt --check -p mantle -p crunch-store`, and TigerStyle exited 0
(selection Run 16). The other runs behind checked tasks predate the fix and
were not rerun.

Task status after Run 56, the final quality gates, and the backend preflight
fix: T3.4 is checked from Run 14 (its assertions pass again in Runs 35, 38, and
47), T2.15 from Runs 26 and 29, T3.3 from Run 36, T2.13 from Run 38 (again in
Run 47), T2.5 from Run 47, Runs 44 to 46, and the code review for T2.5, T3.6
from Run 48 (again in Run 49 at the current test source), T3.5 from Run 51, and
T2.10 from Runs 44 to 47, 55, and 56. Of the runs behind these checked tasks,
only the `store_gc_cli` suite ran again after the fix (see the note above).
Every other task stays open. The closest gaps:

- T2.16: a fresh-process probe with no `mantle/castore/` root for the output's
  node.
- T2.3 and T2.14: a policy file made invalid after open, a build signed by a
  `--signing-key` outside the trust set, a substituted output signed only by a

- T2.11: a Nario v2 batch of exactly 1,024 paths in one Casita revision, and

## Run 57: in-session Casita root/trust recheck and ActionResultPort, 2026-10-04

The regression fixture `casita::tests::cached_node_rechecks_revoked_policy_in_same_session`
first admits a signed Casita output, populates the session node cache, revokes
its signer in the destination policy, asserts a second lookup on the *same*
handle fails with `casita-signer-untrusted`, then restores that signer and
asserts the same handle can read the output again. This is a policy transition,
not a mocked PathInfo response.

(ID 258 when launched) ran `cargo test -p crunch-store --lib

## Run 58: guarded Casita GC, all-entry recovery, and real CLI, 2026-10-04

In the isolated GC branch, a failing-before fenced recovery fixture first
removed the first of two signed roots and had an independent Casita client
repoint the second. Recovery correctly reported `casita-root-conflict` for
the second root, but prematurely deleted the first root's export:
`read_link` at `gc.rs:4079` failed `NotFound` after a successful pre-recovery
`read_link` at line 4073. The Pueue pre-fix task exited Failed(101);
the exact transcript remains at
`/home/brittonr/.cargo-target/mantle-casita-gc-runs-20261004/pre-fix/run.log`,
SHA-256 `4e2e25c0f77da7b4dd594525a4243cc2a71da0eaabe09070a64db56c9231c385`.

The private fix preflights **every** fenced target before cleaning any
removed-root export or index. It derives whether Rust unit cache payload GC
is enabled from the selected backend profile, and always binds the output
root target map into Casita plan identity even while that profile capability
is false. Pueue `mantle-casita-gc-final-36f199ed` (ID 258 at launch)
completed Success: the exact conflicting-later-root fixture `1 passed;
0 failed`, then `gc::tests::casita_` `19 passed; 0 failed` with

## Run 61: integrated admission race, guarded GC recovery, late Nario staging, and GNU linker diagnosis, 2026-10-05

Pueue283 ended with exit 1, **not** a source-built Rust provider. Its
authenticated mrustc-to-1.90 seed reached the Rust 1.91.1 stage, where
`rustc_driver` linked through the generated GNU `cc` with
`-B<sysroot>/lib/rustlib/x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu/bin/gcc-ld -fuse-ld=lld`.
The Rust bootstrap default installed self-contained `rust-lld`, while
Mantle's emitted LLVM configuration deliberately set `LLVM_ENABLE_ZLIB=OFF`.
That LLD rejected real `ELFCOMPRESS_ZLIB` debug sections in the source-built
host objects; no debug information was stripped and no output was published.
`runs/rust-1941-gnu-host-source-rerun-20261005/run.log` SHA-256:
`eeb4ec261d6870dd2ccd99c75df611d34bbe4c5ae0aefb1c2a243aa269adf7ab`;
its `scratch/rustc-stage1-build.log` SHA-256:
`deeedffd216c6cdb339e9dc20b5609b07627703c1743c81de80a173cfd8aec10`.
The generator now emits `[rust] lld = false` and `use-lld = false` only for
the GNU host, leaving the explicit musl-target cross-linker in place. The
executed generated-x.py fixture parsed the emitted TOML and passed one
focused test; `runs/rust-gnu-no-lld-generator-focused-20261005/run.log`
SHA-256 `2ec2348be8e761ba02c89f65517f9effdfded18000cb0dadb568ce94f44f1fa5`.

no-clobber full source-build and smoke run was scheduled as Pueue287, with

accounts for their introduction; the reopened snapshot proves none
was dropped. This proves the batch is one Casita root-publication
revision at the pinned dependency contract, **not** that the global
metadata revision increases by one: unrooted staging can advance it,
and Casita exposes no historical per-root revision field/API. Other

records the initial aggregate **exit 1**, not a passing full run:

All eight positive/negative exact-pin controls returned 0: mismatched
